# file opened: p127_layout.asm
   1++6BDE              ; ============================================================================
   2++6BDE              ; Project127 ATM Core — linked kernel image layout measurement
   3++6BDE              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++6BDE              ;
   5++6BDE              ; Measures the actual assembled Stage2 kernel span [p127_entry,p127_end).
   6++6BDE              ; It is not a fixed reservation: the linked end label determines the size.
   7++6BDE              ; The current loader contract places the image at 6000h and bounds it by A000h.
   8++6BDE              ; Both live host pages used by that CPU window (P5 and P2) must be RESERVED.

   1++6C7A              ; ============================================================================
   2++6C7A              ; Project127 — resident-window contract / software preflight
   3++6C7A              ; Module version: 0.4.0
   4++6C7A              ; Defines the only first-stage remap candidate without touching hardware.
   5++6C7A              ; W1/P5 owns resident execution+data; W2/P2 owns resident stack/control.
   6++6C7A              ; W3 is the only candidate target and may be used only after UI quiescence.
   7++6C7A              ; W0 is ROM. No OUT instruction is permitted here.
   8++6C7A              ; ============================================================================
   9++6C7A              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  10++6C7A              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  11++6C7A              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  12++6C7A
  13++6C7A              ; The remap-critical resident module is deliberately placed first after the
  14++6C7A              ; Stage2 boot body. W1/P5 is the fixed execution/data window; W2/P2 is the
  15++6C7A              ; fixed stack window; W3 is the only target. This preflight checks the linked
  16++6C7A              ; addresses and current live stack at runtime. It performs no page OUT.
  17++6C7A              p127_resident_start:
  18++6C7A
  19++6C7A              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW  EQU 1
  20++6C7A              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_PAGE    EQU 5
  21++6C7A              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW EQU 2
  22++6C7A              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_PAGE   EQU 2
  23++6C7A              P127_RESIDENT_TARGET       EQU 3
  24++6C7A              P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED  EQU 1
  25++6C7A
  26++6C7A              ; IN: A=target window, B=resident code window, C=resident stack window,
  27++6C7A              ;     D=flags (bit 0 set means UI is quiesced).
  28++6C7A              ; OUT: carry=0 if the fixed resident contract is satisfied; carry=1 otherwise.
  29++6C7A              ; This is a software contract check, not a physical mapping operation.
  30++6C7A              p127_resident_validate:
  31++6C7A 32 04 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_target),a
  32++6C7D 78                   ld      a,b
  33++6C7E 32 05 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_code),a
  34++6C81 79                   ld      a,c
  35++6C82 32 06 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_stack),a
  36++6C85 7A                   ld      a,d
  37++6C86 32 07 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_flags),a
  38++6C89
  39++6C89 3A 04 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  40++6C8C FE 03                cp      P127_RESIDENT_TARGET
  41++6C8E C2 B8 6C             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  42++6C91 3A 05 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_code)

  45++6C99 3A 06 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_stack)

  50++6CA6 CA B8 6C             jp      z,p127_resident_reject

  52++6CA9 3A 04 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)

  54++6CAE CA B8 6C             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  55++6CB1 FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  56++6CB3 CA B8 6C             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  57++6CB6 B7                   or      a
  58++6CB7 C9                   ret
  59++6CB8
  60++6CB8              p127_resident_reject:
  61++6CB8 37                   scf
  62++6CB9 C9                   ret
  63++6CBA
  64++6CBA              ; Synthetic software-only tests: accepted W3 with UI quiesced; reject W1,

 348++6EAB              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 349++6EAB              ; Stage 2.10 — real-address resident trampoline preflight (software only).
 350++6EAB              ; Requires DI on entry; DI is asserted again and interrupts remain disabled.
 351++6EAB              ; Z80 does not provide a general instruction to read/restore prior IFF1, so
 352++6EAB              ; this entry contract intentionally does not attempt an EI/IFF round trip.
 353++6EAB              ; W1 is #4000-#7FFF; W2 is #8000-#BFFF. The linked resident module must fit
 354++6EAB              ; wholly in W1, all resident state is inside that module, and the live stack
 355++6EAB              ; must be in W2. No page register is written.

 364++6EB8

 367++6EBE B7                   or      a
 368++6EBF ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 369++6EC1 D2 F8 6E             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 370++6EC4
 371++6EC4                      ; Representative mutable state must be within the resident module.
 372++6EC4 21 A5 6E             ld      hl,p127_w3_state

 374++6ECA B7                   or      a
 375++6ECB ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 376++6ECD DA F8 6E             jp      c,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 377++6ED0

 380++6ED6 B7                   or      a
 381++6ED7 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 382++6ED9 D2 F8 6E             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 383++6EDC
 384++6EDC                      ; CALL has already pushed its return address. The live stack must
 385++6EDC                      ; still be within W2, independent of W3's mapping.

   1++6EFA              ; ============================================================================
   2++6EFA              ; Project127 — interrupt safety contract / preflight
   3++6EFA              ; Module version: 0.5.0
   4++6EFA              ; This is deliberately a DI-only bootstrap contract, not an IRQ dispatcher.
   5++6EFA              ; No EI, IM instruction, vector write, or hardware page OUT is permitted.
   6++6EFA              ; ============================================================================
   7++6EFA              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++6EFA              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MINOR EQU 5
   9++6EFA              P127_IRQ_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++6EFA              P127_IRQ_MASKED EQU 1
  11++6EFA              P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE EQU 0
  12++6EFA              P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET EQU 0
  13++6EFA
  14++6EFA              ; Boot owns the CPU in a DI-protected phase. A stock Z80 cannot read IM0/1/2
  15++6EFA              ; directly; LD A,I exposes IFF2 in P/V but is not a general IFF restore path.
  16++6EFA              ; Therefore this module never guesses prior interrupt state and never EI/IM.
  17++6EFA              ; Until a real vector table/handler is installed, vector ownership is NONE.
  18++6EFA              ; Interrupts may not be enabled until all three prerequisites are explicitly
  19++6EFA              ; established by later stages: writable/owned vector table, verified table
  20++6EFA              ; mapping, and installed resident handler. No current boot code sets these.
  21++6EFA              P127_IRQ_READY_VECTOR EQU 1
  22++6EFA              P127_IRQ_READY_MAP EQU 2
  23++6EFA              P127_IRQ_READY_HANDLER EQU 4
  24++6EFA              P127_IRQ_READY_ALL EQU 7
  25++6EFA
  26++6EFA              p127_irq_init:
  27++6EFA F3                   di
  28++6EFB 3E 01                ld      a,P127_IRQ_MASKED
  29++6EFD 32 DC 71             ld      (p127_irq_mask_state),a
  30++6F00 AF                   xor     a
  31++6F01 32 DD 71             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
  32++6F04 32 DE 71             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a
  33++6F07 32 DF 71             ld      (p127_irq_ready_flags),a
  34++6F0A B7                   or      a
  35++6F0B C9                   ret
  36++6F0C

  38++6F0C              p127_irq_validate:

  40++6F0F FE 01                cp      P127_IRQ_MASKED

  78++6F5F              ; and resident handler are all explicitly committed. This routine NEVER EI.

   1++71E0              ; ============================================================================
   2++71E0              ; Project127 ATM Core — physical page-state manager
   3++71E0              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4++71E0              ; ABI: reserve A=first page, B=count; alloc -> A=page, C=exhausted;
   5++71E0              ;      free A=page, C=invalid/not allocated.
   6++71E0              ; Model: 64 physical 16 KiB page IDs = exactly 1 MiB.
   7++71E0              ; ATM Turbo 2+ page IDs are physical 16 KiB RAM pages. Bootstrap reservations
   8++71E0              ; reflect the active ZX-128 map: page 5 (window 1), page 2 (window 2), and
   9++71E0              ; page 0 (window 3). Page 5 is already covered by the video-page union.
  10++71E0              ; ============================================================================
  11++71E0              P127_MEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++71E0              P127_MEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  13++71E0              P127_MEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3

   1++735E              ; ============================================================================
   2++735E              ; Project127 ATM Core — UINT32 physical range validator
   3++735E              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++735E              ; ABI:
   5++735E              ;   p127_range_start  = 4-byte little-endian physical start address
   6++735E              ;   p127_range_length = 4-byte little-endian byte length
   7++735E              ;   call p127_range_validate; carry=1 if invalid, clear if valid; clobbers AF, B, DE, HL
   8++735E              ; Semantics: half-open [start, start+length), bounded by 1 MiB.
   9++735E              ; Zero-length ranges are valid when start <= 1 MiB.
  10++735E              ; This is arithmetic/accounting only; it does not map or access RAM.
  11++735E              ; ============================================================================
  12++735E              P127_RANGE_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  13++735E              P127_RANGE_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  14++735E              P127_RANGE_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  15++735E              P127_RANGE_LIMIT         EQU 0100000h
  16++735E
  17++735E              p127_range_validate:
  18++735E                      ; Add the unsigned 32-bit length to the unsigned start.
  19++735E 21 6C 74             ld      hl,p127_range_start
  20++7361 11 70 74             ld      de,p127_range_length
  21++7364 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  22++7365 47                   ld      b,a
  23++7366 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  24++7367 80                   add     a,b
  25++7368 32 74 74             ld      (p127_range_end),a
  26++736B 23                   inc     hl
  27++736C 13                   inc     de
  28++736D 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  29++736E 47                   ld      b,a
  30++736F 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  31++7370 88                   adc     a,b
  32++7371 32 75 74             ld      (p127_range_end+1),a
  33++7374 23                   inc     hl
  34++7375 13                   inc     de
  35++7376 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  36++7377 47                   ld      b,a
  37++7378 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  38++7379 88                   adc     a,b
  39++737A 32 76 74             ld      (p127_range_end+2),a
  40++737D 23                   inc     hl
  41++737E 13                   inc     de
  42++737F 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  43++7380 47                   ld      b,a
  44++7381 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  45++7382 88                   adc     a,b
  46++7383 32 77 74             ld      (p127_range_end+3),a
  47++7386 D8                   ret     c                       ; unsigned 32-bit addition overflow
  48++7387
  49++7387                      ; End-exclusive address must be <= 0x00100000.
  50++7387 3A 77 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end+3)
  51++738A B7                   or      a
  52++738B C2 A9 73             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  53++738E 3A 76 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end+2)
  54++7391 FE 10                cp      010h
  55++7393 DA A7 73             jp      c,p127_range_valid
  56++7396 C2 A9 73             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  57++7399 3A 75 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end+1)
  58++739C B7                   or      a
  59++739D C2 A9 73             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  60++73A0 3A 74 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end)
  61++73A3 B7                   or      a
  62++73A4 C2 A9 73             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  63++73A7              p127_range_valid:
  64++73A7 B7                   or      a
  65++73A8 C9                   ret
  66++73A9              p127_range_invalid:
  67++73A9 37                   scf
  68++73AA C9                   ret

  70++73AB              ; Regression tests exercise the 1 MiB boundary, zero length and uint32 wrap.
  71++73AB              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failed assertion.
  72++73AB              p127_range_selftest:
  73++73AB                      ; [0, 0) is valid.
  74++73AB AF                   xor     a

  87++73BC 23                   inc     hl

   1++7478              ; ============================================================================
   2++7478              ; Project127 ATM Core — top-down physical program-page arena
   3++7478              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++7478              ;
   5++7478              ; Arena discovers the highest contiguous FREE physical run from the page-state
   6++7478              ; map. The current reservation map yields P8..P63; P6 remains isolated below
   7++7478              ; reserved P7. Physical pages are 16 KiB and P63 ends at 0x100000.
   8++7478              ;
   9++7478              ; API:
  10++7478              ;   p127_arena_init: discovers and validates the highest contiguous FREE run.
  11++7478              ;   p127_arena_alloc: IN B=page count; OUT A=first page, carry=1 on failure.
  12++7478              ;                    Allocates downward from the current high boundary.
  13++7478              ;   p127_arena_free: IN A=first page, B=count; only most-recent block accepted.
  14++7478              ;                   Frees it and contracts the high boundary upward.
  15++7478              ;
  16++7478              ; This is page-granular accounting only. It does not map pages, access RAM,
  17++7478              ; provide byte-granular allocation, or promise a seamless legacy 16-bit ABI.
  18++7478              ; Free is intentionally LIFO until per-process ownership/coalescing exists.
  19++7478              ; ============================================================================
  20++7478              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  21++7478              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  22++7478              P127_ARENA_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  23++7478              P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE      EQU 64
  24++7478              P127_ARENA_PAGE_SIZE      EQU 04000h
  25++7478
  26++7478              ; Discover the highest contiguous FREE run from the actual page-state map.
  27++7478              ; The first RESERVED/ALLOCATED page below that run is the lower guard.
  28++7478              ; An all-FREE map is rejected: required system reservations are missing.
  29++7478              p127_arena_init:
  30++7478 21 5D 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state+P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  31++747B 0E 3F                ld      c,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1

  37++7484 2B                   dec     hl
  38++7485 0D                   dec     c
  39++7486 10 F7                djnz    .p127_arena_init_scan
  40++7488 C3 3E 75             jp      p127_arena_reject
  41++748B              .p127_arena_init_guard_found:

  45++748F D2 3E 75             jp      nc,p127_arena_reject

  49++7498 CA 3E 75             jp      z,p127_arena_reject
  50++749B 32 D0 75             ld      (p127_arena_page_count),a
  51++749E 3E 40                ld      a,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  52++74A0 32 D1 75             ld      (p127_arena_next_page),a
  53++74A3 B7                   or      a
  54++74A4 C9                   ret
  55++74A5

   1++75D6              ; ============================================================================
   2++75D6              ; Project127 ATM Core — managed logical address-space page table
   3++75D6              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++75D6              ;
   5++75D6              ; 4 MiB logical space: 256 logical pages x 16 KiB. A logical page maps to one
   6++75D6              ; allocator-owned physical ATM page (0..63). Translation metadata only:
   7++75D6              ; no CPU-window mapping, physical byte access, IN/OUT or DMA.
   8++75D6              ;
   9++75D6              ; API:
  10++75D6              ;   p127_as_init: clear all slots to FFh (unmapped).
  11++75D6              ;   p127_as_bind: A=logical page 0..255, B=allocated physical page 0..63.
  12++75D6              ;   p127_as_unbind: A=logical page; removes metadata, does not free RAM.
  13++75D6              ;   p127_as_translate: p127_as_address (uint32 LE) -> A=physical page,
  14++75D6              ;                      HL=14-bit page offset; carry=1 if invalid/unmapped.
  15++75D6              ; Caller must unbind before releasing a physical page.
  16++75D6              ; ============================================================================
  17++75D6              P127_AS_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  18++75D6              P127_AS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  19++75D6              P127_AS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  20++75D6              P127_AS_LOGICAL_PAGE_COUNT EQU 256
  21++75D6              P127_AS_PAGE_SIZE EQU 04000h
  22++75D6              P127_AS_UNMAPPED EQU 0FFh
  23++75D6
  24++75D6              p127_as_init:
  25++75D6 21 77 77             ld      hl,p127_as_page_table
  26++75D9 36 FF                ld      (hl),P127_AS_UNMAPPED
  27++75DB 11 78 77             ld      de,p127_as_page_table+1
  28++75DE 01 FF 00             ld      bc,P127_AS_LOGICAL_PAGE_COUNT-1
  29++75E1 ED B0                ldir

  34++75E5              p127_as_bind:

  40++75F1 5F                   ld      e,a

  46++75FB C2 89 76             jp      nz,p127_as_reject

  56++760E

  95++7644              ; Page index = address >> 14; offset = address & 3FFFh.

   1++7A57              ; ============================================================================
   2++7A57              ; Project127 — managed logical-span copy
   3++7A57              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7A57              ;
   5++7A57              ; Copies between non-overlapping mapped logical spans using the guarded W3
   6++7A57              ; lease and a resident 64-byte bounce buffer. W1/P5 holds this code/buffer;
   7++7A57              ; W2/P2 holds the live stack. No UI call occurs while W3 is leased.
   8++7A57              ;
   9++7A57              ; API inputs:
  10++7A57              ;   p127_as_copy_source      uint32 little-endian
  11++7A57              ;   p127_as_copy_destination uint32 little-endian
  12++7A57              ;   p127_as_copy_length      uint32 little-endian
  13++7A57              ; Outputs:
  14++7A57              ;   carry clear = success; carry set = failure
  15++7A57              ;   p127_as_copy_completed   exact bytes written before return
  16++7A57              ;   p127_as_copy_error       0 success, 1 span, 2 overlap, 3 lease,
  17++7A57              ;                            4 source map, 5 destination map, 6 lease restore
  18++7A57              ; Overlap is rejected; this is not memmove. Unexpected runtime failures after
  19++7A57              ; writes begin may leave a destination prefix modified.
  20++7A57              ; ============================================================================

   2++7FD3              ; Project127 ATM Core — pure software page-mapper model

  13++7FD3              ; Shadow map reflects the verified bootstrap layout:
  14++7FD3              ; W0=TR-DOS ROM, W1=RAM P5, W2=RAM P2, W3=RAM P0.
  15++7FD3              ; This table is descriptive state only; it does not program hardware.
  16++7FD3              p127_map_init:

  18++7FD5 32 C9 81             ld      (p127_map_shadow),a

  27++7FE9

  39++7FF4

  41++7FF4              ; IN: A=window 0..3. OUT: HL=port; carry=1 for invalid window.
  42++7FF4              ; This routine returns an address only; it never performs I/O.

   1++82F5              ; ============================================================================
   2++82F5              ; Project127 — resident stack handoff probe
   3++82F5              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++82F5              ; Runs from the resident Stage2 image in W1/P5. Scratch stack is a bounded
   5++82F5              ; test area in the still-mapped W2/P2. No page port is written.
   6++82F5              ; ============================================================================
   7++82F5              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++82F5              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
   9++82F5              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++82F5
  11++82F5              ; Current bootstrap stack is at #BFFF in W2/P2. Keep at least 128 bytes
  12++82F5              ; between its low-water boundary and the scratch stack.
  13++82F5              P127_HANDOFF_MIN_OLD_SP    EQU 0B900h
  14++82F5              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_BOTTOM  EQU 0B800h
  15++82F5              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_TOP     EQU 0B880h
  16++82F5              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO      EQU 0B7FEh
  17++82F5              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI      EQU 0B7FFh
  18++82F5
  19++82F5              ; IN: none.
  20++82F5              ; OUT: carry=1 on failure; carry=0 on success.
  21++82F5              ; Preserves BC, DE, HL, IX and IY. AF/flags are return status.
  22++82F5              ; Requires interrupts disabled. DI is asserted defensively and this routine
  23++82F5              ; returns with interrupts disabled. IM and interrupt vectors are untouched.
  24++82F5              ; The active stack must be at/above #B900 after this routine's save frame.
  25++82F5              ; This is a stack-integrity probe only: W2 remains mapped throughout.
  26++82F5              p127_handoff_stack_probe:
  27++82F5 F3                   di
  28++82F6 F5                   push    af
  29++82F7 C5                   push    bc
  30++82F8 D5                   push    de
  31++82F9 E5                   push    hl
  32++82FA DD E5                push    ix
  33++82FC FD E5                push    iy
  34++82FE ED 73 8D 83          ld      (p127_handoff_saved_sp),sp
  35++8302
  36++8302                      ; Refuse if the live stack has crossed its declared low-water limit.
  37++8302 21 00 00             ld      hl,0
  38++8305 39                   add     hl,sp
  39++8306 11 00 B9             ld      de,P127_HANDOFF_MIN_OLD_SP
  40++8309 B7                   or      a
  41++830A ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++830C DA 5D 83             jp      c,p127_handoff_stack_refuse
  43++830F

  45++830F 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h
  46++8311 32 FE B7             ld      (P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO),a
  47++8314 3E 5A                ld      a,05Ah
  48++8316 32 FF B7             ld      (P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI),a
  49++8319

  51++8319 31 80 B8             ld      sp,P127_HANDOFF_STACK_TOP

  38+ A011              p126_atm_video_init:
  38+ A011
  39+ A011 F5                   push    af
  40+ A012 C5                   push    bc
  41+ A013 D5                   push    de
  42+ A014 E5                   push    hl
  43+ A015
  44+ A015                      ; Reset the ATM paging/control latch BEFORE enabling permanent
  45+ A015                      ; DOSEN. On this board #EFF7 is part of the ATM paging control path;
  46+ A015                      ; resetting it after the #0177 shadow-port setup can undo the state
  47+ A015                      ; needed by the TR-DOS overlay.
  48+ A015 AF                   xor     a
  49+ A016 01 F7 EF             ld      bc, #EFF7
  50+ A019 ED 79                out     (c), a
  51+ A01B
  52+ A01B                      ; ROM2=1 (D4) is required for TR-DOS overlay; D3 selects alternate
  53+ A01B                      ; video pages 7/3. This is #18, matching the host display setup.
  54+ A01B 3E 18                ld      a, #18
  55+ A01D 01 FD 7F             ld      bc, #7FFD
  56+ A020 ED 79                out     (c), a
  57+ A022 32 3B 5C             ld      (#5C3B), a
  58+ A025
  59+ A025                      ; Open shadow ports and hold DOSEN permanently (A9=0) using the
  60+ A025                      ; documented TR-DOS ROM trampoline. Do this AFTER the paging reset.
  61+ A025 01 35 A0             ld      bc, p126_atm_ports_open_done
  62+ A028 C5                   push    bc
  63+ A029 01 53 2A             ld      bc, #2A53
  64+ A02C C5                   push    bc
  65+ A02D 01 77 01             ld      bc, #0177
  66+ A030 3E C3                ld      a, #C3
  67+ A032 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
  68+ A035
  69+ A035              p126_atm_ports_open_done:
  70+ A035                      ; Window 0 must use ROM with D7=1 so its ROM page selection is
  71+ A035                      ; coupled to ROM2/DOSEN. #BF selects the low ROM page with the
  72+ A035                      ; DOSEN-controlled low ROM select: BASIC-48 when DOSEN=0, TR-DOS
  73+ A035                      ; when DOSEN=1. This is the ATM2+ documented auto-TR-DOS mapping.
  74+ A035 3E BF                ld      a, #BF
  75+ A037 01 F7 3F             ld      bc, #3FF7
  76+ A03A ED 79                out     (c), a
  77+ A03C
  78+ A03C
  79+ A03C                      ; Map the alternate screen pages while the 32-byte mode trampoline
  80+ A03C                      ; is installed at #FFEC. Window 3 uses a fixed RAM page here; the
  81+ A03C                      ; TR-DOS auto-select mux is configured separately in window 0.
  82+ A03C 3E 78                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE7
  83+ A03E 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
  84+ A041 ED 79                out     (c), a
  85+ A043 3E 7C                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE3
  86+ A045 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
  87+ A048 ED 79                out     (c), a
  88+ A04A
  89+ A04A                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p126_atm_video_mode_ready
  90+ A04A 21 EC FF             ld      hl, #FFEC
  91+ A04D 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  92+ A04F 23                   inc     hl
  93+ A050 36 77                ld      (hl), #77
  94+ A052 23                   inc     hl
  95+ A053 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  96+ A055 23                   inc     hl
  97+ A056 36 ED                ld      (hl), #ED
  98+ A058 23                   inc     hl
  99+ A059 36 79                ld      (hl), #79

 113+ A06E              p126_atm_video_mode_ready:
 114+ A06E                      ; Restore CPU-visible host/staging pages. Keep D7 clear on #FFF7 so

  93  A3F2                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_physmem.asm"
# file opened: p127_physmem.asm
   1+ A3F2              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A3F2              ; Project127 physical ATM RAM page test — Stage 2.23
   3+ A3F2              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4+ A3F2              ; Runs from W2 (#A000..), with stack in W2. W1 resident/kernel remains fixed.
   5+ A3F2              ; W3 is exclusively leased from the UI and restored to physical page 0.
   6+ A3F2              ; This is the first general-purpose physical W3 remap test.
   7+ A3F2              ; ============================================================================
   8+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  10+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  11+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  12+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE   EQU 0C000h
  13+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT     EQU 0FFF7h
  14+ A3F2
  15+ A3F2              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failure. Starts with two pages, then allocates and
  16+ A3F2              ; scans every remaining free physical page under one W3 ownership lease.
  17+ A3F2              ; The probe byte is complemented so it can never equal the saved P0 byte.
  18+ A3F2              ; If the dispatcher is not actually switching, only that one P0 byte is
  19+ A3F2              ; touched and it is restored before returning. Full-page writes start only
  20+ A3F2              ; after the physical switch has been proven.
  21+ A3F2              p127_physmem_batch_selftest:
  22+ A3F2 3E 53                ld      a,'S'
  23+ A3F4 CD A0 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace
  24+ A3F7 AF                   xor     a
  25+ A3F8 32 63 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_error),a
  26+ A3FB 32 64 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_alloc_count),a
  27+ A3FE 32 65 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_quiesced),a
  28+ A401 32 66 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_acquired),a
  29+ A404 32 67 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_mapped),a
  30+ A407 32 68 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_valid),a
  31+ A40A
  32+ A40A                      ; The host/staging page must be the documented W3 page before entry.
  33+ A40A 3A CC 81             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
  34+ A40D B7                   or      a
  35+ A40E C2 A5 A6             jp      nz,p127_physmem_fail_map_state
  36+ A411
  37+ A411 CD 5D 72             call    p127_mem_alloc
  38+ A414 DA AA A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_alloc
  39+ A417 32 69 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_page_a),a
  40+ A41A 32 71 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_page_list),a
  41+ A41D 3E 01                ld      a,1
  42+ A41F 32 64 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_alloc_count),a
  43+ A422
  44+ A422 CD 5D 72             call    p127_mem_alloc
  45+ A425 DA AA A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_alloc
  46+ A428 32 6A AB             ld      (p127_physmem_page_b),a
  47+ A42B 32 72 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_page_list+1),a
  48+ A42E 3E 02                ld      a,2
  49+ A430 32 64 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_alloc_count),a
  50+ A433
  51+ A433 3A 69 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_a)
  52+ A436 47                   ld      b,a
  53+ A437 3A 6A AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_b)
  54+ A43A B8                   cp      b
  55+ A43B CA AA A6             jp      z,p127_physmem_fail_alloc
  56+ A43E
  57+ A43E                      ; No UI routine may run while W3 is reassigned.
  58+ A43E CD 38 6D             call    p127_w3_quiesce
  59+ A441 DA AF A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_lease
  60+ A444 3E 01                ld      a,1
  61+ A446 32 65 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_quiesced),a
  62+ A449 CD 57 6D             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
  63+ A44C DA AF A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_lease
  64+ A44F 3E 01                ld      a,1
  65+ A451 32 66 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_acquired),a
  66+ A454
  67+ A454                      ; Preserve one byte from P0 for a non-destructive port-open probe.
  68+ A454 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
  69+ A457 32 6C AB             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_p0),a
  70+ A45A 2F                   cpl

 420+ A736              p127_physmem_cleanup_done:

 438+ A75D              p127_physmem_cleanup_return:

 440+ A760 B7                   or      a
 441+ A761 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error
 442+ A763                      ; The live page sweep ran with W3 exclusively leased, so progress was
 443+ A763                      ; mirrored to #EF only. Replay the compact page list now that W3=P0
 444+ A763                      ; and the UI owner has been restored.
 445+ A763 CD 6A A7             call    p127_physmem_replay_page_log

 447+ A767 C9                   ret
 448+ A768              p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error:
 449+ A768 37                   scf
 450+ A769 C9                   ret
 451+ A76A
 452+ A76A              ; Replay all successful physical-page selections on the visible ATM console.
 453+ A76A              ; The 57 IDs are compacted into two wrapped lines rather than 114 long lines.
 454+ A76A              p127_physmem_replay_page_log:
 455+ A76A                      ; The W3 sweep and its cleanup may leave the video-bank selector in a
 456+ A76A                      ; different state even though the CPU windows have been restored.
 457+ A76A                      ; Reapply the proven Project126/ATM RG6 setup only after the W3 lease
 458+ A76A                      ; is released and UI ownership is active. This preserves screen RAM.
 459+ A76A CD 11 A0             call    p126_atm_video_init
 460+ A76D
 461+ A76D                      ; Fixed-row probe: if this label is visible, the selected alternate
 462+ A76D                      ; text bank (P7 characters / P3 attributes) and PrintAt path agree.
 463+ A76D 21 14 A8             ld      hl,p127_physmem_screen_video_line

 570+ A89F              p127_physmem_cleanup_error:

 573+ A8A4 37                   scf
 574+ A8A5 C9                   ret
 575+ A8A6
 576+ A8A6              ; Check the seven fixed reservation states: P0/P1/P2/P3/P4/P5/P7.
 577+ A8A6              ; OUT carry=1 if any protected page is not RESERVED.
 578+ A8A6              p127_physmem_check_reserved:
 579+ A8A6 21 1E 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 580+ A8A9 06 08                ld      b,8
 581+ A8AB 0E 00                ld      c,0
 582+ A8AD              p127_physmem_reserved_loop:
 583+ A8AD 79                   ld      a,c
 584+ A8AE FE 06                cp      6
 585+ A8B0 28 2E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 586+ A8B2 FE 07                cp      7
 587+ A8B4 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 588+ A8B6 FE 00                cp      0
 589+ A8B8 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 590+ A8BA FE 01                cp      1
 591+ A8BC 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 592+ A8BE FE 02                cp      2
 593+ A8C0 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 594+ A8C2 FE 03                cp      3
 595+ A8C4 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 596+ A8C6 FE 04                cp      4
 597+ A8C8 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 598+ A8CA FE 05                cp      5
 599+ A8CC 28 02                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 600+ A8CE 18 10                jr      p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 601+ A8D0              p127_physmem_reserved_check:
 602+ A8D0 C5                   push    bc
 603+ A8D1 E5                   push    hl
 604+ A8D2 59                   ld      e,c
 605+ A8D3 16 00                ld      d,0
 606+ A8D5 21 1E 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 607+ A8D8 19                   add     hl,de
 608+ A8D9 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 609+ A8DA FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 610+ A8DC E1                   pop     hl
 611+ A8DD C1                   pop     bc
 612+ A8DE 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reserved_fail
 613+ A8E0              p127_physmem_reserved_skip:
 614+ A8E0 0C                   inc     c
 615+ A8E1 10 CA                djnz    p127_physmem_reserved_loop
 616+ A8E3 B7                   or      a
 617+ A8E4 C9                   ret
 618+ A8E5              p127_physmem_reserved_fail:
 619+ A8E5 37                   scf
 620+ A8E6 C9                   ret
 621+ A8E7
 622+ A8E7              ; Every page in the private list must remain ALLOCATED during the sweep.
 623+ A8E7              p127_physmem_check_allocated:
 624+ A8E7 3A 64 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 625+ A8EA 47                   ld      b,a
 626+ A8EB 21 71 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 627+ A8EE              p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop:
 628+ A8EE 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 629+ A8EF 5F                   ld      e,a
 630+ A8F0 16 00                ld      d,0
 631+ A8F2 C5                   push    bc
 632+ A8F3 E5                   push    hl
 633+ A8F4 21 1E 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 634+ A8F7 19                   add     hl,de
 635+ A8F8 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 636+ A8F9 FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 637+ A8FB E1                   pop     hl
 638+ A8FC C1                   pop     bc
 639+ A8FD 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail
 640+ A8FF 23                   inc     hl
 641+ A900 10 EC                djnz    p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop
 642+ A902 B7                   or      a
 643+ A903 C9                   ret
 644+ A904              p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail:
 645+ A904 37                   scf
 646+ A905 C9                   ret
 647+ A906
 648+ A906              ; After release, every non-reserved page must be FREE; reserved pages
 649+ A906              ; are checked separately by p127_physmem_check_reserved.
 650+ A906              p127_physmem_check_free_state:
 651+ A906 21 1E 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 652+ A909 06 40                ld      b,64
 653+ A90B 0E 00                ld      c,0
 654+ A90D              p127_physmem_free_state_loop:
 655+ A90D 79                   ld      a,c
 656+ A90E FE 00                cp      0
 657+ A910 28 1E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 658+ A912 FE 01                cp      1
 659+ A914 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 660+ A916 FE 02                cp      2
 661+ A918 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 662+ A91A FE 03                cp      3
 663+ A91C 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 664+ A91E FE 04                cp      4
 665+ A920 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 666+ A922 FE 05                cp      5
 667+ A924 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 668+ A926 FE 07                cp      7
 669+ A928 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 670+ A92A 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 671+ A92B B7                   or      a
 672+ A92C 20 0D                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 673+ A92E 18 05                jr      p127_physmem_free_state_next
 674+ A930              p127_physmem_free_state_reserved:
 675+ A930 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 676+ A931 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 677+ A933 20 06                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 678+ A935              p127_physmem_free_state_next:
 679+ A935 23                   inc     hl
 680+ A936 0C                   inc     c
 681+ A937 10 D4                djnz    p127_physmem_free_state_loop
 682+ A939 B7                   or      a
 683+ A93A C9                   ret
 684+ A93B              p127_physmem_free_state_fail:
 685+ A93B 37                   scf
 686+ A93C C9                   ret
 687+ A93D
 688+ A93D              ; Emit one printable phase marker directly on the established #EF debug port.
 689+ A93D              ; This remains available while the UI is quiesced and W3 is leased.
 690+ A93D
 691+ A93D              ; ============================================================================
 692+ A93D              ; Stage 2.20 — guarded physical-page API.
 693+ A93D              ; All code and stack remain in W2. The only hardware mapping operation is W3.
 694+ A93D              ; Public ABI:
 695+ A93D              ;   alloc_page: OUT A=allocated page, carry=1 if exhausted.
 696+ A93D              ;   free_page:  IN A=allocated page, carry=1 if invalid or lease active.
 697+ A93D              ;   w3_begin:  quiesce UI, acquire W3, save P0 byte; DI remains in force.
 698+ A93D              ;   w3_map:    IN A=allocated page; valid only inside an active W3 lease.
 699+ A93D              ;   w3_end:    restore W3=P0 and saved byte, release lease, resume UI.
 700+ A93D              ; No API maps W0/W1/W2, frees a currently mapped page, or enables IRQs.
 701+ A93D              ; ============================================================================
 702+ A93D              p127_physmem_alloc_page:
 703+ A93D C3 5D 72             jp      p127_mem_alloc
 704+ A940
 705+ A940              p127_physmem_free_page:
 706+ A940 32 9D AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 707+ A943 3A 98 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 708+ A946 B7                   or      a
 709+ A947 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 710+ A94A 3A 9D AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 711+ A94D 47                   ld      b,a
 712+ A94E 3A CC 81             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 713+ A951 B8                   cp      b
 714+ A952 CA FA A9             jp      z,p127_physmem_api_reject
 715+ A955 3A 9D AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 716+ A958 C3 73 72             jp      p127_mem_free
 717+ A95B
 718+ A95B              ; Begin a bounded W3 lease. Bootstrap mapping must be W3=P0.
 719+ A95B              p127_physmem_w3_begin:
 720+ A95B F3                   di
 721+ A95C 3A 98 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 722+ A95F B7                   or      a
 723+ A960 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 724+ A963 3A CC 81             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 725+ A966 B7                   or      a
 726+ A967 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 727+ A96A CD 38 6D             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 728+ A96D D8                   ret     c
 729+ A96E 3E 01                ld      a,1
 730+ A970 32 99 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 731+ A973 CD 57 6D             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
 732+ A976 38 15                jr      c,p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback
 733+ A978 3E 01                ld      a,1
 734+ A97A 32 9A AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 735+ A97D 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 736+ A980 32 9C AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_p0),a
 737+ A983 3E 01                ld      a,1
 738+ A985 32 9B AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 739+ A988 32 98 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 740+ A98B B7                   or      a
 741+ A98C C9                   ret
 742+ A98D              p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback:
 743+ A98D CD 7F 6D             call    p127_w3_resume
 744+ A990 AF                   xor     a
 745+ A991 32 99 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 746+ A994 37                   scf
 747+ A995 C9                   ret
 748+ A996
 749+ A996              ; Map only an allocator-owned page into W3.
 750+ A996              p127_physmem_w3_map:
 751+ A996 32 9D AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 752+ A999 3A 98 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 753+ A99C FE 01                cp      1
 754+ A99E C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 755+ A9A1 3A 9D AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 756+ A9A4 FE 40                cp      P127_PHYSMEM_MAX_PAGES
 757+ A9A6 D2 FA A9             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_reject
 758+ A9A9 5F                   ld      e,a
 759+ A9AA 16 00                ld      d,0
 760+ A9AC 21 1E 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 761+ A9AF 19                   add     hl,de
 762+ A9B0 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 763+ A9B1 FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 764+ A9B3 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 765+ A9B6 3A 9D AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 766+ A9B9 C3 26 AB             jp      p127_physmem_set_w3
 767+ A9BC
 768+ A9BC              ; End the lease in recovery order. If release/resume fails, state flags are
 769+ A9BC              ; retained so the caller can retry this routine without losing ownership.
 770+ A9BC              p127_physmem_w3_end:
 771+ A9BC 3A 98 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 772+ A9BF FE 01                cp      1
 773+ A9C1 C2 FA A9             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 774+ A9C4 AF                   xor     a
 775+ A9C5 CD 26 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 776+ A9C8 D8                   ret     c
 777+ A9C9 3A 9B AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_valid)
 778+ A9CC B7                   or      a
 779+ A9CD 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_release
 780+ A9CF 3A 9C AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_p0)
 781+ A9D2 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 782+ A9D5              p127_physmem_w3_end_release:
 783+ A9D5 3A 9A AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_acquired)
 784+ A9D8 B7                   or      a
 785+ A9D9 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_resume
 786+ A9DB CD 6B 6D             call    p127_w3_handoff_release
 787+ A9DE D8                   ret     c
 788+ A9DF AF                   xor     a
 789+ A9E0 32 9A AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 790+ A9E3              p127_physmem_w3_end_resume:
 791+ A9E3 3A 99 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_quiesced)
 792+ A9E6 B7                   or      a
 793+ A9E7 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_done
 794+ A9E9 CD 7F 6D             call    p127_w3_resume
 795+ A9EC D8                   ret     c
 796+ A9ED AF                   xor     a
 797+ A9EE 32 99 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 798+ A9F1              p127_physmem_w3_end_done:
 799+ A9F1 AF                   xor     a
 800+ A9F2 32 9B AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 801+ A9F5 32 98 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 802+ A9F8 B7                   or      a
 803+ A9F9 C9                   ret
 804+ A9FA
 805+ A9FA              p127_physmem_api_reject:
 806+ A9FA 37                   scf
 807+ A9FB C9                   ret
 808+ A9FC
 809+ A9FC              ; Runtime API contract test. It proves rejection paths, allocated-page-only
 810+ A9FC              ; mapping, mapped-page free protection, P0 byte restoration and UI recovery.
 811+ A9FC              p127_physmem_api_selftest:
 812+ A9FC AF                   xor     a
 813+ A9FD 32 98 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 814+ AA00 32 99 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 815+ AA03 32 9A AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 816+ AA06 32 9B AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 817+ AA09 3E 01                ld      a,1
 818+ AA0B CD 40 A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 819+ AA0E D2 96 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 820+ AA11 CD 3D A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 821+ AA14 DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 822+ AA17 32 9E AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_page),a
 823+ AA1A 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 824+ AA1D 32 9F AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_p0),a
 825+ AA20 CD 5B A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_begin
 826+ AA23 DA 90 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_free
 827+ AA26                      ; A reserved page must never be accepted by the map API.
 828+ AA26 3E 01                ld      a,1
 829+ AA28 CD 96 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 830+ AA2B D2 8D AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 831+ AA2E 3A 9E AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 832+ AA31 CD 96 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 833+ AA34 DA 8D AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 834+ AA37                      ; The currently mapped page cannot be freed while the lease is held.
 835+ AA37 3A 9E AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 836+ AA3A CD 40 A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 837+ AA3D D2 8D AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 838+ AA40 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h
 839+ AA42 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 840+ AA45 CD BC A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 841+ AA48 DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 842+ AA4B 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 843+ AA4E 47                   ld      b,a
 844+ AA4F 3A 9F AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_p0)
 845+ AA52 B8                   cp      b
 846+ AA53 C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 847+ AA56 3A 9E AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 848+ AA59 CD 40 A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 849+ AA5C DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 850+ AA5F                      ; First-fit must return the same page after release.
 851+ AA5F CD 3D A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 852+ AA62 DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 853+ AA65 47                   ld      b,a
 854+ AA66 3A 9E AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 855+ AA69 B8                   cp      b
 856+ AA6A C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 857+ AA6D 78                   ld      a,b
 858+ AA6E CD 40 A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 859+ AA71 DA 96 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 860+ AA74 3A A5 6E             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 861+ AA77 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 862+ AA79 C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 863+ AA7C 3A A6 6E             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 864+ AA7F FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 865+ AA81 C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 866+ AA84 3A CC 81             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 867+ AA87 B7                   or      a
 868+ AA88 C2 96 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 869+ AA8B B7                   or      a
 870+ AA8C C9                   ret

 890+ AAA0              p127_physmem_trace:
 891+ AAA0 01 EF 00             ld      bc,00EFh
 892+ AAA3 ED 79                out     (c),a
 893+ AAA5 C9                   ret
 894+ AAA6
 895+ AAA6              ; Emit progress for each physical page while the UI is quiesced.
 896+ AAA6              ; A='F' prints FILL PAGE ID=xx; A='V' prints VERIFY PAGE ID=xx.
 897+ AAA6              ; Preserve every register because the caller owns the page-list cursor.
 898+ AAA6              p127_physmem_trace_page:
 899+ AAA6 F5                   push    af
 900+ AAA7 C5                   push    bc
 901+ AAA8 D5                   push    de
 902+ AAA9 E5                   push    hl
 903+ AAAA FE 46                cp      'F'
 904+ AAAC 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_trace_page_verify
 905+ AAAE 21 F8 AA             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_fill_text
 906+ AAB1 18 03                jr      p127_physmem_trace_page_text
 907+ AAB3              p127_physmem_trace_page_verify:
 908+ AAB3 21 06 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_verify_text
 909+ AAB6              p127_physmem_trace_page_text:
 910+ AAB6 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 911+ AAB7 B7                   or      a
 912+ AAB8 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_trace_page_value
 913+ AABA 23                   inc     hl
 914+ AABB E5                   push    hl
 915+ AABC CD F2 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 916+ AABF E1                   pop     hl
 917+ AAC0 18 F4                jr      p127_physmem_trace_page_text
 918+ AAC2              p127_physmem_trace_page_value:
 919+ AAC2 3A 6F AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)
 920+ AAC5 CD D7 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_hex8
 921+ AAC8 3E 0D                ld      a,13
 922+ AACA CD F2 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 923+ AACD 3E 0A                ld      a,10
 924+ AACF CD F2 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 925+ AAD2 E1                   pop     hl
 926+ AAD3 D1                   pop     de
 927+ AAD4 C1                   pop     bc
 928+ AAD5 F1                   pop     af
 929+ AAD6 C9                   ret
 930+ AAD7
 931+ AAD7              p127_physmem_trace_hex8:
 932+ AAD7 F5                   push    af
 933+ AAD8 E6 F0                and     0F0h
 934+ AADA 0F                   rrca
 935+ AADB 0F                   rrca
 936+ AADC 0F                   rrca
 937+ AADD 0F                   rrca
 938+ AADE CD E7 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_nibble
 939+ AAE1 F1                   pop     af
 940+ AAE2 E6 0F                and     00Fh
 941+ AAE4 C3 E7 AA             jp      p127_physmem_trace_nibble
 942+ AAE7
 943+ AAE7              p127_physmem_trace_nibble:
 944+ AAE7 5F                   ld      e,a
 945+ AAE8 16 00                ld      d,0
 946+ AAEA 21 16 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_hex_table
 947+ AAED 19                   add     hl,de
 948+ AAEE 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 949+ AAEF C3 F2 AA             jp      p127_physmem_trace_char
 950+ AAF2
 951+ AAF2              p127_physmem_trace_char:
 952+ AAF2 01 EF 00             ld      bc,00EFh
 953+ AAF5 ED 79                out     (c),a
 954+ AAF7 C9                   ret
 955+ AAF8
 956+ AAF8              p127_physmem_trace_fill_text:
 957+ AAF8 46 49 4C 4C          db      "FILL PAGE ID=",0
 957+ AAFC 20 50 41 47
 957+ AB00 45 20 49 44
 957+ AB04 3D 00
 958+ AB06              p127_physmem_trace_verify_text:
 959+ AB06 56 45 52 49          db      "VERIFY PAGE ID=",0
 959+ AB0A 46 59 20 50
 959+ AB0E 41 47 45 20
 959+ AB12 49 44 3D 00
 960+ AB16              p127_physmem_trace_hex_table:
 961+ AB16 30 31 32 33          db      "0123456789ABCDEF"
 961+ AB1A 34 35 36 37
 961+ AB1E 38 39 41 42
 961+ AB22 43 44 45 46
 962+ AB26
 963+ AB26              ; IN A=physical RAM page ID. Program only W3 (#FFF7), never W0/W1/W2.
 964+ AB26              ; D6=1 selects RAM, D7=0 selects the inverse D0-D5 page ID.
 965+ AB26              p127_physmem_set_w3:
 966+ AB26 32 6B AB             ld      (p127_physmem_page_arg),a
 967+ AB29 CD E9 7F             call    p127_map_encode_page
 968+ AB2C D8                   ret     c
 969+ AB2D 01 F7 FF             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT
 970+ AB30 ED 79                out     (c),a
 971+ AB32 3A 6B AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_arg)
 972+ AB35 32 CC 81             ld      (p127_map_shadow+3),a
 973+ AB38 B7                   or      a
 974+ AB39 C9                   ret
 975+ AB3A
 976+ AB3A              ; IN D=pattern seed. Byte(address)=ROL(address_low) XOR address_high XOR D.
 977+ AB3A              ; The same address produces a different expected value at each seed.
 978+ AB3A              p127_physmem_fill_pattern:
 979+ AB3A 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE
 980+ AB3D 01 00 40             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE
 981+ AB40              .p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop:
 982+ AB40 7D                   ld      a,l
 983+ AB41 07                   rlca
 984+ AB42 AC                   xor     h
 985+ AB43 AA                   xor     d
 986+ AB44 77                   ld      (hl),a
 987+ AB45 23                   inc     hl
 988+ AB46 0B                   dec     bc
 989+ AB47 78                   ld      a,b
 990+ AB48 B1                   or      c
 991+ AB49 20 F5                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop
 992+ AB4B C9                   ret
 993+ AB4C
 994+ AB4C              ; IN D=pattern seed. Verify all 16 KiB against the address-derived pattern.
 995+ AB4C              p127_physmem_verify_pattern:
 996+ AB4C 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE
 997+ AB4F 01 00 40             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE
 998+ AB52              .p127_physmem_verify_pattern_loop:
 999+ AB52 7D                   ld      a,l
1000+ AB53 07                   rlca
1001+ AB54 AC                   xor     h
1002+ AB55 AA                   xor     d
1003+ AB56 BE                   cp      (hl)
1004+ AB57 20 08                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_verify_pattern_fail
1005+ AB59 23                   inc     hl
1006+ AB5A 0B                   dec     bc
1007+ AB5B 78                   ld      a,b
1008+ AB5C B1                   or      c
1009+ AB5D 20 F3                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_verify_pattern_loop
1010+ AB5F B7                   or      a
1011+ AB60 C9                   ret
1012+ AB61              .p127_physmem_verify_pattern_fail:
1013+ AB61 37                   scf
1014+ AB62 C9                   ret
